- **selbstwirksamkeit-aufbau**: Entwirf eine Beherrschungs-Erfahrungs-Sequenz, die systematisch Schüler:innen-Vertrauen in eine Fähigkeit aufbaut, die sie vermeiden. Verwende diesen Skill, wenn SuS sagen ‚Ich kann das nicht', Aufgaben vermeiden oder gelernte Hilflosigkeit zeigen. ([luuspoo-create/claude-bildungs-skills](https://github.com/luuspoo-create/claude-bildungs-skills/tree/HEAD/schule-wohlbefinden/selbstwirksamkeit-aufbau))
- **selcy-audit**: Run /selcy-audit on an existing codebase with no context/ bundle. Reads the real code and reverse-engineers the context/ bundle from it, so /selcy-implement, /selcy-check, /selcy-test and /selcy-sync work on a project you did not start from scratch. Observes, never invents. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-audit) · ★1)
- **selcy-check**: Run /selcy-check before closing a feature. verify mode runs the real app and proves the feature behaves per its spec's Check When Done; review mode reads the diff on a fresh model and ranks findings. Read only, never edits code. Points failures at /selcy-debug or /selcy-implement. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-check) · ★1)
- **selcy-debt**: Run /selcy-debt when you find a bug that is not yours, or a gotcha the next chat needs. Records pre-existing bugs with the feature that owns them, and per-module gotchas, in context/debt.md so the current chat stays clean and the finding has a home. Read only on the debt file. Never fixes anything. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-debt) · ★1)
- **selcy-debug**: Run /selcy-debug when something is broken. Reproduces the bug on demand, narrows it to the smallest failing surface, changes one thing at a time, fixes the root cause with the smallest possible change, and hands a regression test to /selcy-test. No features, no extra refactors. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-debug) · ★1)
- **selcy-grill**: Run /selcy-grill on any decision in a running project. Interviews you relentlessly across the design tree until every branch is resolved, then writes the decision down where the bundle keeps it. The interview primitive behind /selcy-sdd, usable on its own. Use it before a change whose design is still open. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-grill) · ★1)
- **selcy-implement**: Run /selcy-implement to build one feature spec into working code. Reads the context/ bundle (AGENTS.md plus the context files it names) and the one feature spec, gates on input coverage so nothing load bearing is invented mid build, builds, runs the spec's Check When Done, updates progress-tracker.md, and commits. The builder half of /selcy-sdd. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-implement) · ★1)
- **selcy-orchestrate**: Turns an approved /selcy-sdd spec bundle into a kanban board of feature cards, then works them one at a time as the dispatcher spawns it. Each card delegates one feature to `opencode run`, resumes that same session if the build is interrupted instead of starting a new one, runs an independent review session and an independent test session, verifies against the spec's Check When Done, and completes or blocks. Fully autonomous once the bundle is approved — the board is the trigger. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/orchestrator/selcy-orchestrate) · ★1)
- **selcy-sdd**: Run /selcy-sdd once at project start. Interviews you in structured rounds (combining /scope product questions with /architect technical questions), then batch generates the full SDD context bundle plus numbered feature specs. Safe to resume in a new chat. Each feature spec is then built with /selcy-implement in its own chat. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-sdd) · ★1)
- **selcy-sync**: Run /selcy-sync after you edit a context/ file by hand, or after a feature lands, to keep the bundle honest. Reconciles progress-tracker.md against the repo, detects a context file that drifted from the feature specs, and flags specs the change made stale. Surgical edits only: adds lines, rewrites single lines it owns. Never a whole section, never curated prose. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-sync) · ★1)
- **selcy-test**: Run /selcy-test after /selcy-implement builds a feature, to write the test suite the feature deserves. Scopes from git uncommitted changes, reads the context/ testing standards to know what must be covered first, and picks the right strategy per file: happy path, edge cases, error states, accessibility. Saves the framework choice so it never asks twice. ([maulanaadib/skills](https://github.com/maulanaadib/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/software-engineer/selcy-test) · ★1)
- **seldon**: Independent plan/spec reviewer — reads a plan file, verifies claims against the workspace, and returns a structured verdict with findings. Supports external judges (Codex, OpenAI, etc.) or runs inline. ([degrammer/seldon](https://github.com/degrammer/seldon/tree/HEAD/skills/seldon) · ★14)
- **seldon**: Phillips Seldon design system — React components in src/components and src/patterns, co-located Storybook and Vitest, SCSS with $px BEM prefix. Use for library work, Agent Review on UI components, and code review in this repo. Canonical project rules live here plus AGENTS.md and docs/agents/*. ([PhillipsAuctionHouse/seldon](https://github.com/PhillipsAuctionHouse/seldon/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/seldon) · ★6)
- **seldon**: Seldon knowledge transfer specialist — dispatcher for teaching, assessment, harvesting, and curriculum delivery ([GuitarAlchemist/Demerzel](https://github.com/GuitarAlchemist/Demerzel/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/seldon) · ★2)
- **seldon**: REQUIRED before changing this machine when Seldon keeps its logbook (a `seldon` command on PATH). Seldon records every package, service and config change, and why; an agent accounts for its work in a case. Use when installing, removing or upgrading software (pacman, yay, makepkg, `omarchy pkg`, `omarchy update`), running sudo or pkexec, enabling services, editing files under ~/.config, taking snapper snapshots, or when asked about cases, drift, the logbook or why something is on this machine, and after diagnosing a crash (the report goes into the logbook).
